Explanatory Remarks:

1. No cleaning-up process is applied.

saṭīkāmarakośasya [nāṭyavarga: rājā bhaṭṭārako devastatsutā bhartṛdārikā || devī kṛtābhiṣekāyāmitarāsu tu bhaṭṭinī || 13 || abrahmaṇyamavadhyoktau rājaśālastu rāṣṭriyaḥ || ambā mātā'tha bālā syābāsūrāryastu māriṣaḥ|| 14 || attikā bhaginī jyeṣṭhā niṣṭhānirvahaṇe same|| haṇḍe halle halāhāne nīcāṃ ceṭīṃ sakhī prati || 15|| bhadārikā ekam | kṛto'bhiṣeko yaskhāstasāṃ rāśyāṃ devītyekam | bhūriprayogā ye yeṣu paryāyeṣvapi teṣu te || 1 ||
ākāśe tridive nāko lokastu bhuvane jane ||
padye yaśasi ca ślokaḥ śare khaṅge ca sāyakaḥ || 2 ||
jambukau kroṣṭuvaruṇau pṛthukau cipiṭārbhakau ||
ālokau darśanadyotau bherīpaṭahamānakau || 3 ||
utsaṅgacihnayoraṅkaḥ kalaṅkoṅkāpavādayoḥ ||
(takṣako nāgavardhakyorarkaḥ sphaṭikasūryayoḥ ) || 4 || ( 1 )

vakṣyamāṇeṣu kāntādivargeṣveva ke'pi nānārthā uktā natu prāguktaparyāyeṣu | yathā | mārute vedhasi bradhne puṃsi kaḥ kaṃ śiro'mbunoriti | bhūriprayogā yatra kutrāpi kāvyādiṣu kavibhiḥ prācuryeṇa prayuktā ye nākalokādayaḥ śabdāste pūrvokteṣu yeṣu paryāyeṣu dṛśyaṃte teṣu paryāyeṣu apiśabdādatrāpi kāntādiṣu kīrtitāḥ | yathā nākaśabdaḥ pracuraprayogatvātprāk svargākāśayoruktopi punarihoktaḥ| jambukaśabdastu sṛgālaparyāyeṣvevokto natu varuṇaparyāyeṣu | bhūriprayogatvābhāvāt | iha tu jambukau kroṣṭuvaruṇāvityubhayatrocyate || 1 || nāka ityekaṃ nāma kakārāntaṃ ākāśe svarge ca vartate | na akaṃ duḥkhamatra nākaḥ "namrād" iti sūtre nipātitaḥ | • bhubane svargādau jane ca loka ityekam | "lokyate lokaḥ " | paye anuṣṭuvādau pace yaśasi kīrtau śloka ityekam | ślokyate ślokaḥ | "śloka saṃghāte" | yakṣasi yathā | uttamaloko hariḥ | ścare bāṇe khaḍne ca sāyakaḥ || 2 || koṣṭā sṛgālaḥ baruṇaḥ prasiddha ubhau jammukaśabdavācyau | "jambuka: pherave nīce paścimāśāpatāvapi " iti medinī | cipiṭo bhraṣṭavrīhitaṇḍulaḥ pohe iti laukika bhāṣāyām | arbhakaḥ śiśuḥ tāvubhau pṛthukau | "pṛthukaḥ puṃsi vipiṭe śiśau syādabhidheyavat" iti | darśanaṃ prakāśaālokau | "ālokastu pumān dhote darśane bandibhāṣaṇe" iti medinī | bherīpaṭahaṃ vādyaviśeṣau | tūryāitvādekavadbhāvaḥ | umāvānakau | "ānakaḥ paṭahe bheryā mṛdaṅge dhvanadambude " iti medinī || 3 || utsaṃgaḥ kroḍaḥ tatra cihe cāiḥ | "aṅko rūpakabhredā'vaciharekhājibhūṣaṇe | rūpakāṃśāntikotsaṅgasthāne'ṅkaṃ pāpaduḥkhayoḥ" iti medinī | cihe doṣārope ca kalaṅkaḥ | "kalako ke'pavāde ca kālāyasamalespi ca" iti medinī | nāgaviśeṣe tvaṣṭari va takṣakaḥ | sphaṭikasūryasorarka

mārute veghasi braghne puṃsi kaḥ kaṃ śiro'mbunoḥ ||
syātpulākastucchaghānye saṃkṣepe bhaktasikke || 5 ||
ulūke kariṇaḥ pucchamūlopānte ca pecakaḥ ||
kamaṇḍalau ca karakaḥ sugate ca vināyakaḥ || 6 ||
kiṣkurhaste vitastau ca śukrakīṭe ca vṛścikaḥ ||
pratikūle pratīkastriṣvekadeśe tu puṃsyayam || 7 ||
syāṛtikaṃ tu bhūnimbe kattṛṇe bhūstṛṇe'pi ca ||
jyotsikāyāṃ ca ghoṣe ca kośātakyatha kaṭphale || 8 ||
site ca khadire somavalkaḥ syādaya silhake ||
